# Oanarina UI Tool — feature register

2022 individually named requirements. 1992 required baseline items; 30 later Oanarina improvements.

This is a working parity specification, not a claim of completeness or of implemented Figma compatibility. Reference links identify category-level starting points; each requirement marked reference-verification-pending still needs an in-app or documentation check. Several OS integration, reliability, and cross-product requirements are Oanarina requirements rather than verified Figma functions. Figma Design is the first implementation target; Draw, Dev Mode, FigJam, Slides, Sites, Buzz and Make remain in scope in later phases. Features introduced by a future Figma update require another audit.

Status: {"implemented":1317,"partial":272,"planned":433}. **planned** = not implemented; **partial** = material limits documented; **implemented** = working path with validation evidence. Production parity requires additional accessibility, performance, platform and user acceptance testing even for implemented items. Binary .fig Design import supports a documented subset; binary export remains planned.

Machine-readable source: public/features.json. Regenerate this document with npm run inventory.

No private Figma editable clipboard. docs/NATIVE-CLIPBOARD.md. |

| UI-05-009 | Per-page zoom level | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.9: Independent 0.05x–8x local page zoom, remembered explicit Fit and stored-view startup-fit suppression. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |

Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |

Raster edges 16384px/80M pixels; bounds remain the slice rectangle. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-10-030 | Slice SVG export | implemented | Unreleased source: Slice SVG profiles download actual fixed-region cropped artwork in slice-local axes, with supported text/appearance, layer-ID and presentation options. Real scaled SVG filenames/dimensions, no slice-overlay paint and immutable source are tested. Uses existing renderer/fonts/media limitations; no binary .fig export. docs/SLICE-EXPORT-WORKFLOWS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |

Approximate cubic flattening tolerance 0.25 parent units, 32 contours/512 input or flattened points/4096 output points; mixed open/closed, crossing/touching/reversed/collapsed geometry refuses atomically. docs/VECTOR-OFFSET.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |

Finite nonzero magnitude 0.001–10000 is validated; stale document/page/selection/mode/Motion/camera/blur contexts refuse Apply. Actual exported pixels and one-history persistence are tested. This offsets paths rather than stroke width. docs/VECTOR-OFFSET.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |

Cached bounded hierarchy/control geometry, ownership/context/session/capture guards, 125% UI and actual raster checks. Partial: supported centered solid stroke contours only; no filled-region knife, general network, multilayer/intersection batch or anchor cuts. docs/VECTOR-CUT.md. Native/reference acceptance pending. |

Partial: no arbitrary knife cuts, networks, crossing/touching/open contours, visible strokes, gradient/image paints, effects, masks or animation tracks. Child coordinate boxes are not tightened. Bounded topology and strict derived-state validation refuse atomically. docs/VECTOR-SEPARATION.md; tests/vector-separation.test.ts; tests/e2e/vector-separation.spec.ts. Native and reference acceptance pending. |

Whole-path removal refuses; no filled-region/network/full-layer erasing. docs/VECTOR-ERASER-LASSO.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |

Stroke-centerline erasure scope and whole-path refusal documented. docs/VECTOR-ERASER-LASSO.md. Exact Figma reference/native acceptance remains pending. |

Inspector reports active/unsupported/estimated states; unsupported geometry exactly retains ordinary behavior. Editable Bridge reports omission; CSS is approximate. Evidence and limits: docs/TEXT-WRAPPING.md; tests/text-wrap.test.ts; tests/e2e/text-wrap.spec.ts; mcp/tests/text-wrap.test.ts. Exact Figma/CSS algorithm equivalence and native visual acceptance remain pending. |

Conservative ink bounds and font/fallback limits; editable Bridge reports omitted optical offsets, SVG retains displayed geometry. docs/HANGING-QUOTATION-MARKS.md; core/browser pixels, malformed metadata/retained-context/MCP checks. Native/reference verification pending. |

Fixed/absolute Design children only; incompatible hug/aspect/auto-text suspends, hard min/max clamps, missing/retyped/out-of-range guide or changed owner detaches at last geometry. docs/RESPONSIVE-GUIDE-BINDINGS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-23-028 | Stretch with layout rows | implemented | Unreleased source: Durable optional y-axis row-guide owner/ID/cell span follows real owner height and guide/style edits in shared reflow. Bind/detach, Undo/save, nested constrained descendants and reference remapping are tested including actual rendered size. Same direct-parent fixed/absolute Design scope and suspension/clamp/detach rules as columns; arbitrary nested-track relations remain unfinished. docs/RESPONSIVE-GUIDE-BINDINGS.md. Native/exact-reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-23-029 | Relative frame positioning | implemented | Unreleased source: dedicated Frame X/Y controls express the selected layer layout origin before its own rotation in the nearest frame/component/instance axes, through transformed intervening groups. Guarded atomic descendant translation, post-derivation validation, one Undo/save/reopen and stale drafts are tested. Limits: 1024 ancestors, ±1,000,000 coordinates; singular/managed/flow/derived/protected workspace cases refuse. Responsive guide bindings remain separate unfinished requirements. See docs/FRAME-RELATIVE-POSITIONING.md. Native/reference acceptance remains pending. |
| UI-23-030 | Responsive resize preview | implemented | Unreleased source: independently resizable Design frames preview requested or preset sizes through the shared layout, constraints, guides, text and component derivation. Real isolated before/after SVG artwork, optional overflow reveal, descendant and linked box-change summaries precede explicit Apply/Undo/save/reopen. Locked frames preview read-only. Managed/controller-owned sizes and unsupported imported hierarchy refuse; stale document/selection/mode/font contexts invalidate. Complete project payload cap 16 MB; 500 scene layers/64 ancestors; solver stability and final geometry/policy signature required. docs/RESPONSIVE-RESIZE-PREVIEW.md; core/browser pixels, grid/guide/aspect/text checks. Overflow candidates remain conservative; native and reference acceptance pending. |

## Comments and annotations

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-36-001 | Add canvas comment | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-002 | Comment pin position | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-003 | Comment author display | implemented | File-local threads show saved authors for posts/replies. New messages use an explicit session display name; legacy absent authors stay unknown. No account authentication or author permissions. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-004 | Comment creation timestamp | implemented | New file-local posts/replies save creation timestamps and display localized times; edits retain creation time plus edited timestamp. Legacy absent time remains unrecorded. Validation, Undo and persistence are checked. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-005 | Comment text editing | implemented | Thread panel edits root/reply text through source-bound drafts and ordinary document history, preserving authors/creation times and recording edited time. Stale same-event actions refuse; bounded legacy text is disclosed. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-006 | Delete own comment | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-007 | Reply to comment | implemented | Thread panel posts, edits and deletes bounded file-local replies with explicit local author/time, Undo/save/reopen, per-thread pagination and malformed-input refusal. No mentions, remote delivery or account ownership. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-008 | Mention collaborator | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-36-009 | Resolve comment | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-010 | Reopen comment | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-011 | Filter unresolved comments | implemented | Cross-page local thread panel filters unresolved threads with bounded indexing/search and explicit truncation diagnostics; filtering changes no document history. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-012 | Filter resolved comments | implemented | Cross-page local thread panel filters resolved threads and reopens them through Undoable edits; filtering changes no project. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-013 | Filter comments by author | implemented | Local thread panel filters saved root/reply author names, alongside status/search and result pagination. Legacy unknown identity is explicit; no cloud user picker. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-014 | Jump to comment | implemented | Jump selects the actual page/anchored layer and centers the derived affine pin position using the logical SVG viewport, preserving artwork and Undo. Missing/collapsed anchors use disclosed saved-position fallback. Source/identity/context guards prevent stale retargeting. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-015 | Comment thread panel | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-36-016 | Comment notification | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-36-017 | Comment link | partial | Copy bounded opaque root-thread references and open them in the matching currently open .oui; valid links navigate pages, center actual pins, reset filters and focus paged/resolved threads without changing artwork or Undo. Clipboard fallback and stale asynchronous/retained handler ownership are validated. No public hosting, reply-specific targets, OS URL handler or cross-file automatic lookup. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-LINKS.md. |
| UI-36-018 | Emoji reaction | partial | Eight saved file-local emoji reactions work independently on roots/replies with distinct shared display-name labels, counts, accessible descriptions, one Undo/save and guarded picker/source/local receipts. No authenticated identities, arbitrary Unicode picker, cloud reactions or notifications. Private metadata stays out of artwork, libraries and explicit Figma delivery. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-REACTIONS.md. |
| UI-36-019 | Copy comment text | implemented | Copy root/reply plain text from the local thread panel; browser clipboard refusal exposes a selected read-only text fallback for standard Command-C. No fabricated native node/SVG clipboard representation or expanded IPC. See docs/LOCAL-COMMENT-THREADS.md. |
| UI-36-020 | Comment on prototype | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-36-021 | Comment permissions | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-36-022 | Annotation tool | partial | Inspect offers real layer annotation editing and optional HTML canvas marker navigation, with private note persistence/Undo. A dedicated pointer annotation placement tool is not implemented. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-023 | Annotation text | implemented | Inspect → Layer annotations creates/edits/removes literal plain-text notes with Undo/save/reopen, original-context guards, locks and component defaults/root overrides. Private metadata is excluded from artwork, library and explicit Figma delivery. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-024 | Annotation category | partial | Annotations choose real named/color categories from their owning layer set. Per-layer scoped categories preserve coherent whole-set instance overrides; document-global categories and exact Figma category parity remain unimplemented. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-025 | Annotation categories management | partial | Inspect creates, renames, recolors and deletes local annotation categories with atomic reference cleanup, Undo and persistence. Categories belong to one layer set; document-global category management remains unimplemented. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-026 | Annotated property reference | partial | Property annotations reference eight validated numeric fields and display their live authored values. Unsupported property types and general paint/style/prototype property references remain unimplemented. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-027 | Measurement annotation | partial | Measurement annotations compute transformed authored box width/height/diagonal using translation-independent affine linear vectors; actual geometry changes update values. Arbitrary point/edge and ink measurements remain unimplemented. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-028 | Distance annotation | partial | Distance annotations follow validated/remapped same-page targets and compute live center-to-center distances, with missing-target and insufficient numeric precision diagnostics. Edge-gap/arbitrary-point distances remain unimplemented. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-029 | Developer note | implemented | Inspect creates and edits file-local developer notes, filters them and displays them in cross-page handoff annotations. Validation, context guards, Undo/persistence and component root override semantics apply; no hosted developer assignment. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-36-030 | Annotation visibility | partial | Saved per-layer annotation marker visibility and an ephemeral global canvas marker toggle work with Undo for stored changes. HTML count markers stay outside Artwork exports; full on-canvas annotation text/measurement visibility parity remains unimplemented. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |

See docs/PLATFORM-PROPERTY-INSPECTION.md. |

See docs/PLATFORM-PROPERTY-INSPECTION.md. |

| UI-41-009 | Layer measurements | implemented | Working preview path; validated by core/browser tests or native smoke. Exact reference parity still needs per-feature audit. |
| UI-41-010 | Spacing inspection | partial | Partial: persistent read-only Inspect comparison with page-axis transformed layout gaps/overlaps, offsets, contained insets, searchable target, sampled/base Motion and guarded copy. | UI-41-015 | Applied variable inspection | implemented | Bound field, actual variable, resolved value, effective base property, Web syntax and missing-reference retained literals. See docs/DEV-INSPECTION.md. |

| UI-41-021 | Completed development status | implemented | Inspect marks page or unlocked editable layer completed, resets to no status, filters completed layers and keeps source/instance override semantics. | UI-41-022 | Handoff section list | implemented | Inspect handoff list searches ready/completed pages, sections and layers across the file; status/scope filters, bounded indexing, 50-row pagination and actual page/layer navigation preserve hidden/locked artwork. Source references and private metadata survive .oui; no hosted annotation backend. See docs/LOCAL-LAYER-ANNOTATIONS.md. |
| UI-41-024 | Developer links | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources attaches named file-local HTTP(S) links; explicit isolated open/copy, no automatic requests. | UI-41-025 | Attach issue link | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources → Issue accepts a named validated HTTP(S) issue URL, editable/removable/copyable with Undo/save/reopen. | UI-41-026 | Attach documentation link | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources → Documentation attaches a named validated HTTP(S) documentation URL with explicit open/copy, editing/removal, Undo and persistence. | UI-41-027 | Attach repository link | implemented | Inspect → Developer resources → Repository attaches a named validated HTTP(S) repository URL with explicit open/copy, editing/removal, Undo and persistence. ## Performance and reliability

| ID | Required behavior | Status | Verification / limits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-53-001 | Large document loading | partial | A real multi-megabyte image recovery test passes. Animation export reports progress and supports cancellation, with 1,800-frame / 4,096-edge / 8-megapixel limits. Large-tree virtualization and comprehensive performance work remain. |
| UI-53-002 | Progressive page loading | planned | reference-verification-pending |
| UI-53-003 | Viewport culling | partial | Partial: bounded conservative whole-root-subtree culling on the Design/Whiteboard canvas, with opt-out, selected/editing retention, exact immutable source index and measured viewport. Real omitted DOM/defs plus on/off pixels, pan/zoom/resize/Undo/export checks. Uncertain text/effect/media/Boolean/complex-stroke roots stay rendered; root mask runs and unsupported scenes fall back fully. Shared Artwork/model/exports unchanged. See docs/CANVAS-VIEWPORT-CULLING.md. |

See docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-grid-files-windows.md. Exact Figma reference acceptance remains separate. Native and MCP writers share the exclusive project lock; native saves compare disk SHA-256 and require an explicit choice after external changes. Live renderer synchronization and non-cooperating application locking remain outside this behavior. See docs/MAC-WINDOWS.md and mcp/README.md. |
| UI-59-038 | Durable MCP undo history | partial | Last twenty transaction snapshots support undo in the running server; they are not persisted across restarts. |

## Layer masks

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UI-60-001 | Use layer as mask | implemented | v0.2.0-dev.4: reachable editing, history/persistence where applicable, core/browser checks and universal Mac build/smoke. See docs/VALIDATION-v0.2-svg-masks-selection.md; exact reference acceptance remains separate. Alpha/vector/luminance source modes mask following same-parent siblings until the next mask. Nested vector mode uses opaque geometry; bounds are conservative, and exact Figma nested-composition parity is unverified. |
